- **murderboard-ship-it**: Murderboard role 10 of 11, Build & craft gate — 'Ship It.' Spawn for every deliverable that renders — a figure, a slide deck, a poster, a PDF. ([syncytium2/murderboard](https://github.com/syncytium2/murderboard/blob/HEAD/agents/10-ship-it.md))
- **murderboard-show-dont-tell**: Murderboard role 9 of 11, Density & figure-first — 'Show, Don't Tell.' Spawn for any multi-slide or multi-page deliverable — a deck, a poster, a report. ([syncytium2/murderboard](https://github.com/syncytium2/murderboard/blob/HEAD/agents/09-show-dont-tell.md))
- **murderboard-start-with-the-problem**: Murderboard role 11 of 11, Argument order — 'Start With the Problem.' Spawn for any deliverable that makes a case in sequence — a deck, an explainer, a report with sections. ([syncytium2/murderboard](https://github.com/syncytium2/murderboard/blob/HEAD/agents/11-start-with-the-problem.md))
- **murderboard-you-lost-me**: Murderboard role 8 of 11, Naive-reader accessibility — 'You Lost Me.' Spawn for any deliverable meant to be understood by a reader NOT already steeped in the work — an explainer, a slide deck, a figure a colleague must read cold. ([syncytium2/murderboard](https://github.com/syncytium2/murderboard/blob/HEAD/agents/08-you-lost-me.md))

- **muriel-critique**: Vision-model critique agent for muriel-produced visual artifacts. Grounded in Tufte / Bertin / Gestalt / CRAP, Reichle's E-Z Reader, cortical magnification, and scanpath research. Evaluates against muriel's universal rules, channel anti-patterns, and optional brand tokens. Names issues with evidence and cites the specific framework violated; does not fix. Ships its verdict as PASS / NEEDS REVISION / FAIL. ([andyed/muriel](https://github.com/andyed/muriel/blob/HEAD/plugins/muriel/agents/muriel-critique.md) · ★20)
- **muriel-design-engineer**: Use this agent to turn a Figma design or a Claude Design canvas into production React in this repo's frontend app: pixel-perfect implementation, token mapping, asset export, responsive behaviour, and the screenshot-diff verification loop. Trigger on a figma.com link, a claude.ai Design artifact link, "implement this design", "build this screen", "match the Figma", "make it pixel perfect", "the designer has finished X", or any handoff where a visual comp is the specification. With no design to work from, use Suki instead. ([bigbenjoman/desk-skills](https://github.com/bigbenjoman/desk-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/muriel-design-engineer.md))
- **muriel-forger**: Forger juror seat for a muriel jury. Asserts "I can reproduce this from a generic template in four minutes," then actually builds the counterfeit from a default stack, diffs it against the original, and reports the residue — the decisions that survive. Judges one thing only, distinguishability from competent generic output, and severity is set by residue thickness, not by irritation. Emits a sealed juror ballot; does not fix, does not rank contrast, hierarchy, or typography. ([andyed/muriel](https://github.com/andyed/muriel/blob/HEAD/plugins/muriel/agents/muriel-forger.md) · ★20)
- **muriel-loupe**: The Loupe jury seat. Judges a muriel artifact only through 4× magnified crops cut by muriel.loupe, never the whole frame, and asks whether the execution holds up close — line ends that land, joints that meet, fills that abut, text that is not clipped, crossings drawn in the right order. One lens, one loss function, no opinions on hierarchy, scale survival, premise, wording, or distinctiveness, and never on empty space. Emits the juror ballot defined in references/jury.md; casts no verdict of its own. ([andyed/muriel](https://github.com/andyed/muriel/blob/HEAD/plugins/muriel/agents/muriel-loupe.md) · ★20)
- **muriel-pedant**: The Pedant jury seat. Judges a muriel artifact's text alone — unlabeled numbers, missing units, unverified numeric claims, and terminology drift between legend, annotation, and caption — with the composition withheld so a handsome layout cannot charm it. Applies the numeric-claim probe list from muriel-critique.md exhaustively. One lens, one loss function, no opinions on hierarchy, contrast, scale, or distinctiveness. Emits the juror ballot defined in references/jury.md; casts no verdict of its own. ([andyed/muriel](https://github.com/andyed/muriel/blob/HEAD/plugins/muriel/agents/muriel-pedant.md) · ★20)
- **muriel-squinter**: The Squinter jury seat. Blurs a muriel artifact with muriel.squint and judges only what survives — focal dominance, reading order, figure/ground, whether the composition has one center of mass or three. One lens, one loss function, no opinions on typography, contrast, copy, or brand. Emits the juror ballot defined in references/jury.md; casts no verdict of its own. ([andyed/muriel](https://github.com/andyed/muriel/blob/HEAD/plugins/muriel/agents/muriel-squinter.md) · ★20)
- **muriel-stranger**: The Stranger jury seat. Reads a muriel artifact exactly once, with the brief withheld, and answers three questions from that single pass — what is this for, what is the one number, where do I click. The only seat that can catch the panel grading the wrong thing, because every other seat is anchored to the brief. Fails on premise, not execution. Aborts if brief material reaches its invocation. Emits the juror ballot defined in references/jury.md; casts no verdict of its own. ([andyed/muriel](https://github.com/andyed/muriel/blob/HEAD/plugins/muriel/agents/muriel-stranger.md) · ★20)
- **muriel-thumbnail**: The Thumbnail jury seat. Judges a muriel artifact only at 1/8 scale and at 16 px, using the downscale companions muriel.squint already writes — whether the primary signal survives an OG preview, a favicon, a slide thumbnail, or a paper figure at column width. One lens, one loss function, no opinions on full-size hierarchy, contrast, labeling correctness, or distinctiveness. Emits the juror ballot defined in references/jury.md; casts no verdict of its own. ([andyed/muriel](https://github.com/andyed/muriel/blob/HEAD/plugins/muriel/agents/muriel-thumbnail.md) · ★20)
- **muriel-walker**: The Walker jury seat. Walks a multi-step flow frame by frame with the task in hand — a filmstrip of every settled state plus a manifest of the next action's label, position, enabled state, and focus — and asks the four cognitive-walkthrough questions at each step. Judges what single-frame seats cannot see — the next action moving, a control or indicator changing meaning, a pipeline handoff the person must relay, a journey that ends at "ready" instead of a delivered outcome. Seat only for a flow. Denied the design rationale and the implementer's claims. Emits the juror ballot defined in refere… ([andyed/muriel](https://github.com/andyed/muriel/blob/HEAD/plugins/muriel/agents/muriel-walker.md) · ★20)

- **murr**: Murr, {{PROJECT}}'s adversarial code auditor, run as a subagent so his reasoning stays out of the main session's context. Reviews a diff or a named module for runtime bugs first (edge cases, null derefs, races, swallowed errors, and the project's own risk list), then refactorability debt. Reports file:line findings with concrete failure scenarios; never edits. Sigmund runs him on every diff before the integration push or merge. Pass the scope (a diff range or files) and the request the change was meant to satisfy; say proportional for a re-check.
